Edge\u003c/a\u003e is a lightweight version of Qdrant designed for edge devices and resource-constrained environments. Unlike Qdrant Server, which uses a client-server architecture, Qdrant Edge runs inside the application process. Data is stored and queried locally and can be synchronized with a Qdrant server. It offers the same powerful vector search capabilities as the client-server version but with a smaller footprint, making it ideal for applications that require low latency and offline functionality.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTo get started with Qdrant Edge from Python or Rust, initialize an instance of EdgeShard, which exposes methods to manage data, query it, and restore snapshots. For example:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"from qdrant_edge import payload={\u0026quot;color\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;red\u0026quot;})\n]))\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003efrom\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan payload values.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eRecommendation\u003c/strong\u003e - use positive and negative examples to find similar points.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDiscovery\u003c/strong\u003e - constrain search to a specific region of the vector space.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSearch Relevance Tuning\u003c/strong\u003e - tools for adjusting search results, such as Maximal Marginal Relevance (MMR) and the Relevance Feedback Query.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMultitenancy\u003c/strong\u003e - scalable partitioning of data for multi-user environments.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eObservability\u003c/strong\u003e - comprehensive metrics, telemetry, and audit logging for monitoring and debugging.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eQuery Planning and Payload Indexes\u003c/strong\u003e - leverages stored payload information to optimize query execution strategy.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSIMD Hardware Acceleration\u003c/strong\u003e - utilizes modern CPU x86-x64 and Neon architectures to deliver better performance.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eGPU Support\u003c/strong\u003e - for accelerated indexing, with support for NVIDIA and AMD GPUs.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAsync I/O\u003c/strong\u003e - uses \u003ccode\u003eio_uring\u003c/code\u003e to maximize disk throughput utilization even on network-attached storage.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eWrite-Ahead Logging\u003c/strong\u003e - ensures data persistence with update confirmation, even during power outages.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWeb UI\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca You can connect to embedding providers, AI application frameworks, and data pipeline tools, as well as observability platforms for monitoring and tracing your vector search in production. No-code and low-code automation platforms are supported too. <p align="center" dir="auto">
    <b>Vector Search Engine for the next generation of AI applications</b>
</p>
<p align="center" dir="auto">

</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Qdrant</strong> (read: <em>quadrant</em>) is a vector similarity search engine and vector database.
It provides a production-ready service with a convenient API to store, search, and manage points—vectors with an additional payload.
Qdrant is tailored for extended filtering support, making it useful for all sorts of neural-network or semantic-based matching, faceted search, and other applications.</p>
<p dir="auto">Qdrant is written in Rust 🦀, which makes it fast and reliable even under high load. See <a href="https://qdrant.tech/benchmarks/" rel="nofollow">benchmarks</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">With Qdrant, embeddings or neural network encoders can be turned into full-fledged applications for matching, searching, recommending, and much more!</p>
<p dir="auto">Qdrant is also available as a fully managed <strong><a href="https://cloud.qdrant.io/" rel="nofollow">Qdrant Cloud</a></strong> ⛅ including a <strong>free tier</strong>.</p>
<p align="center" dir="auto">
<strong><a href="https://qdrant.tech/documentation/quickstart/" rel="nofollow">Quick Start</a> • <a href="#agent-skills">Agent Skills</a> • <a href="#clients">Client Libraries</a> • <a href="#demo-projects">Demo Projects</a> • <a href="#integrations">Integrations</a> • <a href="#contacts">Contact</a>
</strong>
</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Getting Started</h2><a id="user-content-getting-started" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Getting Started" href="#getting-started"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Agent Skills</h3><a id="user-content-agent-skills" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Agent Skills" href="#agent-skills"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Qdrant provides a collection of ready-to-use <a href="https://github.com/qdrant/skills">agent skills</a> that bring Qdrant's vector search capabilities directly into your AI coding assistant. Install these skills to empower your agent in making critical engineering decisions for optimal vector search performance, such as quantization, sharding, tenant isolation, hybrid search, model migration, and more.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Client-Server</h3><a id="user-content-client-server" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Client-Server" href="#client-server"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To experience the full power of Qdrant locally, run the container with this command:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="docker run -p 6333:6333 qdrant/qdrant"><pre>docker run -p 6333:6333 qdrant/qdrant</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Note that this starts an insecure deployment without authentication, open to all network interfaces. Please refer to <a href="https://qdrant.tech/documentation/security/#secure-your-instance" rel="nofollow">secure your instance</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Now you can connect to the server with any <a href="#clients">client</a>. For example, using Python:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from qdrant_client import QdrantClient

client = QdrantClient(url=&quot;http://localhost:6333&quot;)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">qdrant_client</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">QdrantClient</span>

<span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">QdrantClient</span>(<span class="pl-s1">url</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"http://localhost:6333"</span>)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Before deploying Qdrant to production, be sure to read our <a href="https://qdrant.tech/documentation/installation/" rel="nofollow">installation</a> and <a href="https://qdrant.tech/documentation/security/" rel="nofollow">security</a> guides.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Clients</h3><a id="user-content-clients" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Clients" href="#clients"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Qdrant offers the following client libraries to help you integrate it into your application stack:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Official:
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://github.com/qdrant/go-client">Go client</a></li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/qdrant/rust-client">Rust client</a></li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/qdrant/qdrant-js">JavaScript/TypeScript client</a></li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/qdrant/qdrant-client">Python client</a></li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/qdrant/qdrant-dotnet">.NET/C# client</a></li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/qdrant/java-client">Java client</a></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Community:
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://github.com/hkulekci/qdrant-php">PHP</a></li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Qdrant Edge</h3><a id="user-content-qdrant-edge" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Qdrant Edge" href="#qdrant-edge"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://qdrant.tech/documentation/edge/" rel="nofollow">Qdrant Edge</a> is a lightweight version of Qdrant designed for edge devices and resource-constrained environments. Unlike Qdrant Server, which uses a client-server architecture, Qdrant Edge runs inside the application process. Data is stored and queried locally and can be synchronized with a Qdrant server. It offers the same powerful vector search capabilities as the client-server version but with a smaller footprint, making it ideal for applications that require low latency and offline functionality.</p>
<p dir="auto">To get started with Qdrant Edge from Python or Rust, initialize an instance of EdgeShard, which exposes methods to manage data, query it, and restore snapshots. For example:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from qdrant_edge import Distance, EdgeConfig, EdgeVectorParams, EdgeShard, Point, UpdateOperation

shard = EdgeShard.create(&quot;./shard&quot;, EdgeConfig(
    vectors={&quot;my-vector&quot;: EdgeVectorParams(size=4, distance=Distance.Cosine)}
))
shard.update(UpdateOperation.upsert_points([
    Point(id=1, vector={&quot;my-vector&quot;: [0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4]}, payload={&quot;color&quot;: &quot;red&quot;})
]))"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">qdrant_edge</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">Distance</span>, <span class="pl-v">EdgeConfig</span>, <span class="pl-v">EdgeVectorParams</span>, <span class="pl-v">EdgeShard</span>, <span class="pl-v">Point</span>, <span class="pl-v">UpdateOperation</span>

<span class="pl-s1">shard</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-v">EdgeShard</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create</span>(<span class="pl-s">"./shard"</span>, <span class="pl-en">EdgeConfig</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">vectors</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>{<span class="pl-s">"my-vector"</span>: <span class="pl-en">EdgeVectorParams</span>(<span class="pl-s1">size</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">4</span>, <span class="pl-s1">distance</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-v">Distance</span>.<span class="pl-c1">Cosine</span>)}
))
<span class="pl-s1">shard</span>.<span class="pl-c1">update</span>(<span class="pl-v">UpdateOperation</span>.<span class="pl-c1">upsert_points</span>([
    <span class="pl-en">Point</span>(<span class="pl-s1">id</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">1</span>, <span class="pl-s1">vector</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>{<span class="pl-s">"my-vector"</span>: [<span class="pl-c1">0.1</span>, <span class="pl-c1">0.2</span>, <span class="pl-c1">0.3</span>, <span class="pl-c1">0.4</span>]}, <span class="pl-s1">payload</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>{<span class="pl-s">"color"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"red"</span>})
]))</pre></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">REST</h3><a id="user-content-rest" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: REST" href="#rest"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Qdrant provides a REST API with an <a href="https://api.qdrant.tech/" rel="nofollow">OpenAPI 3.0 specification</a>, enabling client generation for virtually any framework or programming language.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">gRPC</h3><a id="user-content-grpc" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: gRPC" href="#grpc"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Features</h2><a id="user-content-features" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Features" href="#features"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Dense, Sparse, and Multi Vector Search</h3><a id="user-content-dense-sparse-and-multi-vector-search" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Dense, Sparse, and Multi Vector Search" href="#dense-sparse-and-multi-vector-search"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Qdrant supports dense vectors for semantic similarity, sparse vectors for full-text search, and multivector search for objects with multiple embeddings or late interaction models like ColBERT.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Filtering on Payload</h3><a id="user-content-filtering-on-payload" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Filtering on Payload" href="#filtering-on-payload"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Attach any JSON payload to your vectors and filter on it using a rich set of conditions—keyword matching, full-text, numeric ranges, geo-locations, and more—combined with <code>should</code>, <code>must</code>, and <code>must_not</code> clauses.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Hybrid Search</h3><a id="user-content-hybrid-search" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Hybrid Search" href="#hybrid-search"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Combine multiple vectors in a single query to get the best of semantic understanding and keyword precision, with results merged via configurable fusion strategies, such as Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF) and Distribution-Based Score Fusion (DBSF).</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Vector Quantization and On-Disk Storage</h3><a id="user-content-vector-quantization-and-on-disk-storage" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Vector Quantization and On-Disk Storage" href="#vector-quantization-and-on-disk-storage"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Built-in quantization cuts RAM usage by up to 97% and lets you tune the trade-off between search speed and precision.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Distributed Deployment</h3><a id="user-content-distributed-deployment" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Distributed Deployment" href="#distributed-deployment"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Scale horizontally with sharding and replication, and update or resize collections with zero downtime.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Highlighted Features</h3><a id="user-content-highlighted-features" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Highlighted Features" href="#highlighted-features"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Faceting</strong> - aggregate search results by payload values.</li>
<li><strong>Recommendation</strong> - use positive and negative examples to find similar points.</li>
<li><strong>Discovery</strong> - constrain search to a specific region of the vector space.</li>
<li><strong>Search Relevance Tuning</strong> - tools for adjusting search results, such as Maximal Marginal Relevance (MMR) and the Relevance Feedback Query.</li>
<li><strong>Multitenancy</strong> - scalable partitioning of data for multi-user environments.</li>
<li><strong>Observability</strong> - comprehensive metrics, telemetry, and audit logging for monitoring and debugging.</li>
<li><strong>Query Planning and Payload Indexes</strong> - leverages stored payload information to optimize query execution strategy.</li>
<li><strong>SIMD Hardware Acceleration</strong> - utilizes modern CPU x86-x64 and Neon architectures to deliver better performance.</li>
<li><strong>GPU Support</strong> - for accelerated indexing, with support for NVIDIA and AMD GPUs.</li>
<li><strong>Async I/O</strong> - uses <code>io_uring</code> to maximize disk throughput utilization even on network-attached storage.</li>
<li><strong>Write-Ahead Logging</strong> - ensures data persistence with update confirmation, even during power outages.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Web UI</h3><a id="user-content-web-ui" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Web UI" href="#web-ui"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Web UI provides a visual way to interact with your data and monitor the health of your deployment. It enables you to explore your collections, manage data, interact with the REST API, and more.</p>
<p align="center" dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" href="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/ae2f4ba52494c28364469a93532703d8f0c888bdd269aec5fa8833ea9941690a/68747470733a2f2f716472616e742e746563682f646f63732f67657474696e67737461727465642f7765622d75692e706e67"><img style="width: 75%; max-width: 100%;" src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/ae2f4ba52494c28364469a93532703d8f0c888bdd269aec5fa8833ea9941690a/68747470733a2f2f716472616e742e746563682f646f63732f67657474696e67737461727465642f7765622d75692e706e67" alt="Qdrant Web UI" data-canonical-src="https://qdrant.tech/docs/gettingstarted/web-ui.png"></a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Integrations</h2><a id="user-content-integrations" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Integrations" href="#integrations"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Qdrant integrates with the tools you're already using across every stage of your AI stack. You can connect to embedding providers, AI application frameworks, and data pipeline tools, as well as observability platforms for monitoring and tracing your vector search in production. No-code and low-code automation platforms are supported too. Refer to the <a href="https://qdrant.tech/documentation/ecosystem/" rel="nofollow">Ecosystem page</a> for the complete list.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Contributing</h2><a id="user-content-contributing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Contributing" href="#contributing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">We are happy to receive your contributions! Before opening a pull request, please read our <a href="/qdrant/qdrant/blob/master/docs/CONTRIBUTING.md">Contributing Guide</a>.</p>
